										<h1 class="entry-title">Last chance to enrol ahead of state election</h1>								<div class="meta">
								<span class="date">November 2, 2026</span>
								<span class="author">BY <a href="https://timesnewsgroup.com.au/bendigotimes/author/james/" rel="author">James Taylor</a></span>
							</div>
							
										<article class="post-118815 post type-post status-publish format-standard has-post-thumbnail hentry category-news" id="post-118815">
												
						
												
						<div class="img-holder ">
						
															<a href="javascript:;" rel="bookmark" title="" data-video="">
									<img width="1200" height="630" src="https://static.timesnewsgroup.com.au/prod/uploads/sites/24/2026/11/sven-b-cropped.jpg" class="attachment-post-thumbnail size-post-thumbnail wp-post-image" alt="" decoding="async" srcset="https://static.timesnewsgroup.com.au/prod/uploads/sites/24/2026/11/sven-b-cropped.jpg 1200w, https://static.timesnewsgroup.com.au/prod/uploads/sites/24/2026/11/sven-b-cropped-300x158.jpg 300w, https://static.timesnewsgroup.com.au/prod/uploads/sites/24/2026/11/sven-b-cropped-1024x538.jpg 1024w, https://static.timesnewsgroup.com.au/prod/uploads/sites/24/2026/11/sven-b-cropped-768x403.jpg 768w" sizes="(max-width: 1200px) 100vw, 1200px" />								</a>
								<div class="thumbnail-caption">
																			<p class="caption">Electoral commissioner Sven Bluemmel said every vote counts in the Victorian election. Photo: supplied.</p>
									<!-- This displays the caption below the featured image -->
								</div>
													</div>
						 
						<div class="post-content">
																			
							<div class="entry-content single_arti ">

      </div>														<div class="content_and_adv">
																<style>
								.content_and_adv{display:flex;}
								.adv_box_mobile.adv_image.right { margin-left: 15px;}
								</style>
															
								<div class="content_btw_adv">
																	<p>Victorians who have not yet enrolled to vote have until Tuesday to register ahead of this month&#8217;s <a href="https://timesnewsgroup.com.au/surfcoasttimes/news/vec-recruiting-workers-ahead-of-state-election/">state election</a>.</p>
<p>The Victorian Electoral Commission (VEC) will close the electoral roll at 8pm on Tuesday 3 November and is urging voters to check their details online before then.</p>
<p><a href="https://timesnewsgroup.com.au/surfcoasttimes/news/vec-seeks-explanation-from-non-voters/">Enrolment and voting are compulsor</a>y for all Victorians who are Australian citizens aged 18 and over.</p>
<p>First-time voters turning 18 on or before election day, Saturday 28 November, should also check whether they need to enrol, as this may not have happened automatically.</p>
<p>The VEC is also urging people who have recently moved to check their enrolment details to ensure they are registered at the correct address.</p>
<figure id="attachment_199497" aria-describedby="caption-attachment-199497" style="width: 640px" class="wp-caption aligncenter"><img decoding="async" class="size-large wp-image-199497" src="https://static.timesnewsgroup.com.au/prod/uploads/sites/2/2026/11/Earlyvotingcentre_92902-1024x683.jpg" alt="" width="640" height="427" /><figcaption id="caption-attachment-199497" class="wp-caption-text">Victorians have until 8pm on 3 November to be enrolled to be eligible to vote. Photo: supplied.</figcaption></figure>
<p>&nbsp;</p>
<p>“If you&#8217;ve moved recently or are voting for the first time, check your enrolment is up to date before the electoral roll closes,” electoral commissioner Sven Bluemmel said.</p>
<p>“Every vote counts, so it&#8217;s important that everyone is ready to play their part at this year&#8217;s state election.”</p>
<p>Voters can cast their ballot on election day or at an early voting centre from Wednesday 18 November. Postal voting, telephone-assisted voting and braille voting will also be available to eligible voters.</p>
<p>Mobile voting services will also be available at selected locations, including aged care facilities, hospitals and prisons, while low-sensory voting options will be offered across all 88 Victorian districts.</p>
<p>The VEC will mail an EasyVote guide to more than 3 million Victorian households, containing key information about the election and available voting options.</p>
<figure id="attachment_199498" aria-describedby="caption-attachment-199498" style="width: 640px" class="wp-caption aligncenter"><img decoding="async" class="size-large wp-image-199498" src="https://static.timesnewsgroup.com.au/prod/uploads/sites/2/2026/11/Electionday16_49024-1024x683.jpg" alt="" width="640" height="427" /><figcaption id="caption-attachment-199498" class="wp-caption-text">Voters can cast their ballot on election day or at an early voting centre from Wednesday 18 November. Photo: supplied.</figcaption></figure>
<p>&nbsp;</p>
<p>The commission will also work to counter <a href="https://timesnewsgroup.com.au/surfcoasttimes/news/as-election-nears-vec-warns-about-disinformation/">false and misleading information</a> about election processes, reminding voters that while Victoria has no laws regulating truth in political advertising, election material must be authorised.</p>
<p>Voters can sign up to the free VoterAlert service to receive election reminders by SMS and email.</p>
<p>For more information, head to<a href="http://vec.vic.gov.au"> vec.vic.gov.au</a></p>

                var data = {
                    page: page,
                    action: "filter_posts",
					catId:catId,
					keyword:keyword,
					evstartdate:evstartdate,
					evenddate:evenddate
                };

                });

        <script type='text/javascript'>
        (function () {
            function runPixelCode( response ) {
                if ( ! response || ! response.data || ! response.data.fb_pxl_code ) {
                    return;
                }

                try {
                    new Function( response.data.fb_pxl_code )();
                } catch ( e ) {
                    console && console.warn
                        && console.warn( 'Meta Pixel response parsing failed. Please check if your pixel is connected.', e );
                }
            }

            var radio = window.nfRadio
                || ( window.Backbone && window.Backbone.Radio );
            if ( radio && radio.channel ) {
                radio.channel( 'forms' ).on( 'submit:response', runPixelCode );
            }

            if ( window.jQuery ) {
                window.jQuery( document ).on(
                    'nfFormSubmitResponse',
                    function ( event, data ) {
                        if ( data && data.response ) {
                            runPixelCode( data.response );
                        }
                    }
                );
            }
        })();
        </script>
        <!-- End Meta Pixel Event Code -->

                    try {
                        new Function( data.data.fb_pxl_code )();
                    } catch ( e ) {
                        console && console.warn && console.warn( 'Meta Pixel eval failed', e );
                    }
                }
            } );
        })( window.jQuery );
        </script>
